# BOSS multi-effects

Canonical root brand profiles; all retain explicit verification gaps. Preserved historical evidence remains qualified in each record.

## GT-1B

Bass multi-effects with built-in expression/toe switch. Consolidated and checked2026-10-16. Current manufacturer page marks discontinued; this does not prove all seller stock unavailable.

### GT-1B — current manufacturer checks

| Required field | Verified evidence / remaining uncertainty (2026-10-16) |
|---|---|
| Physical controls / expression | Three knobs,three footswitches,twelve buttons;built-in expression with assignable toe switch. Shared TRS CTL2,3/EXP2 accepts two external switches or one expression pedal. [GT1B-S] |
| Display / body | 132×32 backlit graphic LCD;diagonal,color/touch not specified.305×152×56mm,max74mm;1.3kg including batteries. [GT1B-S] |
| Presets / processing | 99 preset+99 user=198 total.90 effect types are choices,not90 simultaneous blocks;overall simultaneous ceiling remains unverified. [GT1B-S] |
| Ports / power | Quarter-inch input,stereo pair L/MONO,R outputs;TRS external control;stereo-mini AUX/PHONES;USB-B;DC.200mA;four alkaline AA,approximately7h;PSA adapter optional.USB power and explicit voltage/polarity remain unverified here. [GT1B-S] |
| Synth / acoustic | Bass Synth,Defretter,Harmonist documented. [GT1B-S] BASS SIM changes bass pickup/body character,not generic acoustic-guitar simulation. Synth pitch tracking cannot analyze chords reliably. [GT1B-P] |
| Processing categories / choices | FX1 and FX2 independently choose from25 named families;the same family can occupy both. Compressor offers8 models;limiter4. These choices do not add simultaneously to the chain. Remaining category counts/whole-chain ceiling await complete parameter inventory. [GT1B-P] |
| USB / routing | USB processed output goes to computer;computer return mixes at final output stage. Direct monitor can be switched off for host monitoring and resets ON at startup;no assumed dry reamp path. [GT1B-P] |
| Looper / editor / live | 32s looper. [GT1B-S] Owner manual documents USB driver requirement and dedicated patch edit/name/order/backup software;input plug controls power. [GT1B-M] Legacy Windows/macOS Tone Studio evidence retained above;current OS compatibility still requires support-version checks. |
| IR / NAM / capture | User IR,native NAM,converted NAM and proprietary capture import not established;factory cab models alone do not establish imports. |

### GX-1B — current manufacturer checks

| Required field | Current evidence / limits (2026-10-16) |

| Physical controls / expression | Three knobs,three footswitches,fourteen buttons;assignable built-in expression/toe switch. Shared TRS CTL2,3/EXP2 takes up to two switches or external expression. EV-1-WL/FS-1-WL wireless control supported. [GX1B-S] |
| Display / body | Color graphic LCD320×240;diagonal/touch unspecified.307×149×56mm,max73mm;1.2kg,battery-inclusion basis unspecified on inspected specs. [GX1B-S] |
| Processing / memory | Eight simultaneous blocks plus volume/noise suppression;16 AIRD amp types,135 effect types;99 preset+99 user=198 memories. [GX1B-S] |
| Ports / power | Quarter-inch input,L/MONO/R outs;mini phones;TRS CTL/EXP;USB-C;DC.250mA DC/battery,400mA USB;four alkaline AA about5h;PSA-S optional. [GX1B-S] USB supply5V/500mA minimum;USB takes priority over batteries. INPUT plug controls power;ground terminal present. [GX1B-C] |
| Synth / acoustic / category choices | Forty named FX families counted in parameter list;OVERTONE only FX3,so39 available in FX1/2 and40 in FX3. Same eligible algorithm may occupy all three. AC BASS simulates acoustic bass;Bass Synth needs single notes with other strings muted. Remaining subtype/category totals unverified. [GX1B-F] |

| Editor / audio / rhythms | Tone Studio Windows/macOS/iOS/Android;Bluetooth audio and USB recording. Practice metronome/rhythms described within Tone Studio;do not assume a standalone hardware drum machine. [GX1B-S] Drivers,exact USB channels/bitdepth and current OS versions need separate verification. |
| Looper / carryover | 38s mono/19s stereo. [GX1B-S] Carryover requires same effects,types and order in both memories;tails respond to destination settings. This is not unrestricted spillover between arbitrary presets. [GX1B-T] |

### ME-90B — current manufacturer checks

| Required field | Evidence / limits (2026-10-16) |

| Physical controls / expression | Legacy30knobs/8footswitches/4buttons+toe retained;current manufacturer confirms eight switches and expression/toe. No external EXP socket in published connector list;complete rotary recount still pending. [ME90B-S] |
| Processing / memory / acoustic | Dedicated preamp plus six simultaneous effect categories;10 onboard AIRD preamps,61 onboard effects versus93 types in specification with alternatives.36preset+36user=72 memories. Acoustic preamp is optimized for acoustic-electric/upright pickups,not proof of conversion from electric bass. [ME90B-S] |
| Editor / Bluetooth / IR | Tone Studio Windows/macOS;mobile iOS/Android editing and audio streaming require optional BT-DUAL.Three user speaker IRs;not NAM/capture support. [ME90B-S] IR loader launches from Tone Studio;USER1–3 empty at factory. [ME90B-M] IR format/sample-length and native/converted NAM/proprietary capture remain unverified. |
| Display / power / body | Two-character seven-segment LED plus tuning LEDs;diagonal/color/touch unspecified.195mA;fourAA about6h;PSA-S optional.443×220×67mm,max93mm;2.9kg incl.batteries.USB bus power and DC voltage/polarity remain unverified in inspected text. [ME90B-M] |

| Looper / carryover | 38s mono. [ME90B-S] DLY/REV carryover enabled by default and switchable;does not apply to delay in MOD. [ME90B-M] |
| USB / editor requirements | USB recording/playback. [ME90B-S] Tone Studio guide requires dedicated USB driver before software;Windows/Mac installer instructions distinct from firmware. [ME90B-B] Direct monitoring resets on at startup;USB return mixes with processed bass. [ME90B-P] Exact channels/bitdepth/OS compatibility remain unverified. |

### ME-90B — external control and recording followup, 2026-10-18

| Field | Verified detail / qualification | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| External expression | No wired EXP socket in the published connector list. The manual explicitly supports optional EV-1-WL wireless expression and FS-1-WL wireless footswitches. The linked connection-guide page exposes a download agreement without a PDF in retrieved text; detailed wireless setup and firmware requirements remain unchecked. | [Reference manual][ME90B-M] p15; [EV-1-WL connection-guide landing page](https://www.boss.info/global/support/by_product/me-90b/owners_manuals/77f6bba2-2c2d-4a68-87d9-582acd9c311d/) |
| USB audio / MIDI | Named audio endpoints: INPUT and MIX IN into the unit; OUTPUT and DRY OUT from it. These are endpoint names, not a verified channel count or bit depth. USB MIDI supports program changes and CC11 expression, CC82 pedal-effect on/off and CC80 CTL. Dedicated driver required. | [Reference manual][ME90B-M] pp12–13 |
| Routing / Bluetooth | Computer playback reaches main outputs, phones and balanced XLR; optional Bluetooth audio reaches main outputs/phones but **not balanced XLR**. USB loopback can return computer audio to USB OUT. | [Reference manual][ME90B-M] pp12,14 |

### ME-90B — driver and firmware restrictions, 2026-10-18

| Field | Finding / qualification | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| Editor / driver restrictions | Windows driver **1.0.0** supports Windows 10/11; excludes ARM PCs, virtual Windows, Windows running on Mac and Windows 10 Mobile. These are driver restrictions, not a full editor compatibility matrix. USB channel count/bit depth remain unverified; no installer was downloaded. | [BOSS driver requirements](https://www.boss.info/global/support/by_product/me-90b/updates_drivers/5eeefa47-0044-4b2c-ac1b-f2fba7214d1f/) |
| Firmware / known issues | BOSS lists **1.04, May2025**: fixes incorrect CTL pedal LED behavior after patch changes or Tone Studio operations, plus unspecified minor bugs. **1.03, April2024** fixes a BLEND-function bug. Offered units’ installed versions remain unknown; no update performed. | [BOSS ME-90B system history](https://www.boss.info/global/support/by_product/me-90b/updates_drivers/14808c74-0eb6-4444-a829-c49b73e75e1c/) |

**Rejected sibling guide:** the EV-1-WL link in the [Sweetwater ME-90B quickstart](https://www.sweetwater.com/sweetcare/articles/boss-me-90b-bass-multi-effects-pedal-quickstart-guide/) resolves to [EV-1-WL Connection Guide for ME-90](https://static.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/EV-1-WL_ConnectionGuide_ME-90_eng_01_W.pdf), ©2023, cover explicitly ME-90. Its ME-90 firmware≥1.03 threshold and pairing workflow do not verify ME-90B requirements. The exact ME-90B guide remains unresolved.

### ME-90B — published controls and analog levels, 2026-10-18

| Field | Finding / qualification | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| K/F/B | **30 / 8 / 4 + toe**, derived from the manufacturer’s text inventory: preamp6, MOD5, blend1, COMP4, FILTER4, DRIVE4, DLY/REV4, output1, PEDAL FX1 =30 rotaries. MOD includes both “MOD TYPE” and a generic “TYPE” entry; retain this published enumeration pending visual recount. Buttons: EXIT/WRITE/CTL/EDIT. Eight main switches exclude toe, GND LIFT, AMP/LINE and POWER. | [BOSS specifications][ME90B-S] |
| Analog / electrical levels | INPUT/RETURN: −10dBu nominal, +7dBu maximum, 1MΩ. Main outputs, phones, SEND and balanced XLR: −10dBu nominal. Output impedance: main/SEND1kΩ, phones44Ω, XLR600Ω; recommended loads respectively ≥10kΩ, ≥44Ω, ≥600Ω. These are analog levels, not USB resolution or headphone power. | [BOSS specifications][ME90B-S] |
| Conversion / processing | 48kHz; 24-bit A/D with AF method, 32-bit D/A, 32-bit floating-point processing. This does not establish USB bit depth. | [BOSS specifications][ME90B-S] |

### ME-90B — original Tone Studio guide limits, 2026-10-18

| Field | Finding / qualification | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| Editor / firmware dependency | The ©2024 revision01 PC guide explicitly requires **ME-90B firmware≥1.03** for Tone Studio. This is a model-specific **editor** requirement, not the unresolved wireless-pedal firmware threshold. Offline liveset editing is documented, but saving edited memory results requires the unit connected. | [Tone Studio guide][ME90B-B] p1 |
| IR loading / conversion | IR Loader converts selected IR data before loading USER1–3. This is **speaker-IR conversion**, not NAM conversion or capture support. The inspected guide does not publish accepted file formats, sample rates, bit depths or sample/time limits; those remain unknown. | [Tone Studio guide][ME90B-B] p12 |
| Memory / computer library | A liveset holds at most36 memories; Tone Studio holds up to50 livesets, with further sets exported to computer files. Liveset file extension **.tsl**. Export to the unit does not restore memories beyond36. These computer-library limits do not expand the pedal’s36user+36preset onboard memories. | [Tone Studio guide][ME90B-B] pp6–8 |

### ME-90B — parameter-guide category audit, 2026-10-18

| Field | Finding / qualification | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| Category choices | COMP/FX1:11 numbered choices +2 replacements. FILTER/FX2:11+5. DRIVE/SYNTH:11+4. DLY/REV:11+6, including PHRASE LOOP among the11. PREAMP/EQ:11+7, with EQ among the11. Replacements occupy the last numbered position and are selected per memory through Tone Studio; they are not extra simultaneous blocks. | [Parameter guide][ME90B-P] pp2,4,7,9,12 |
| MOD / pedal choices | MOD:6 selector groups, containing13 subtype choices before7 replacement alternatives; slicer’s20 patterns are not20 independent algorithms. PEDAL FX:11 positions, including control targets for other sections; BLEND is a separate direct-mix function. These counting conventions do not reconcile the advertised61/93 totals into a unique algorithm census. | [Parameter guide][ME90B-P] pp13–17 |
| Synth / acoustic distinction | **AC BASS** explicitly simulates acoustic bass, separately from the ACOUSTIC preamp. SYNTH SAW/SQUARE require single-note playing with other strings muted. Three optional GTR preamp types are documented; instrument-view eligibility needs a separate review. | [Parameter guide][ME90B-P] pp2,7,12 |

### ME-90B — guitar/bass comparison membership, 2026-10-18

| Field | Classification / evidence |
|---|---|
| Instrument scope | Included in **both guitar and bass multi-effects comparisons**. The model’s own [Parameter Guide][ME90B-P] p12 explicitly documents **GTR CRUNCH, GTR HI GAIN and GTR MODDED**, selected through Tone Studio as replacements for PREAMP/EQ position11. This is documented guitar processing within an independent multi-effects architecture. The product remains bass-oriented; guitar inclusion does not certify every bass effect’s voicing for guitar. No second catalogue record is created. |

### VE-22 — output and loop operation,2026-10-17

| Field | Finding / qualification | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| I/O / output modes | **MONO** sums internal stereo to both XLR outputs;**STEREO** preserves L/R;**FX/DRY** sends mono dry+effect mix to L and bypass to R. The left side is not isolated wet-only audio. OUTPUT settings apply globally and save automatically. | [Reference p21][VE22-M] |
| Looper / recording and control | Recorded phrases **cannot be saved** in device memory. PERFORM records harmony/effect/echo;CHECK records before those processors for sound adjustment. Built-in loop control requires Memory mode;Manual mode needs external switches assigned LOOP/LOOP CTL. Double-tap within one second stops;hold clears. | [Reference pp22–23][VE22-M] |
| Expression / memory behavior | **EXP HOLD** globally retains the previous memory’s pedal value when enabled;automatically saved. Unlike that system setting,edited sound parameters require MEMORY WRITE before memory change or power-off. | [Reference pp8,21][VE22-M] |

|---|---|---|

Harmony includes doubling,tripling,interval combinations andManual;Effect includes three distortion/modulation combinations;Echo includes four delay/reverb combinations. These are section choices,not simultaneous blocks. Advertised39-type reconciliation andmaximum remain unresolved. | [Official parameter guide][VE22-PG] pp3,7,13 |
| Pitch / harmony limits | Manual harmony exposes two voices,each with11 interval choices spanning octave-down throughunison tooctave-up. Pitchcorrection offersfive modes:smooth,fast,stepped,fixed-note robot anduncorrected shifting;shift range−12…+12. Selecting pitch correction consumes the Effect section ratherthan proving a separate extra block. | [Parameter guide][VE22-PG] pp6–7 |
| Delay / reverb limits | Effect-section shortdelay**1–100ms**;Echo delay**1–2000ms** withmono/stereo-pan choices;reverb**0.1–10s**,pre-delay**0–500ms**. Slicer has20patterns. These parameter ranges arenot memory orblock counts. | [Parameter guide][VE22-PG] pp8,12–14 |

[VE22-PG]: https://static.roland.com/assets/media/pdf/VE-22_parameter_eng01_W.pdf

## VE-8

Separate acoustic-guitar and vocal multi-effects;consolidated/researched2026-10-16;partial verification.

### VE-8 — preserved original comparison

Checked **2026-10-14**. **Partial records**: `?` means unverified, not No. Dimensions are **W×D×H, mm**. K/F/B = rotary controls / main footswitches / separate buttons; rear power, phantom and ground-lift toggles are additional. Used and new prices are separate **USD US-market asking/listing observations**, not sold-price statistics. Tax, shipping and condition matter.

A guitar input used to detect chords does not automatically provide guitar effects. VE-500's instrument THRU sends the **unprocessed guitar signal** to other pedals. VE-8, by contrast, has its own acoustic-guitar processing path.

## GT-1000CORE

Checked **2026-10-15**. This updates an existing record in [BOSS comparison](BOSS.md)<!-- historical-link:boss-010 -->; it is **not a new model**. Earlier technical research remains dated2026-10-14 unless explicitly rechecked here.

### Display

- **One graphic LCD,256×80 pixels**, manufacturer specification. Published diagonal was not located and remains **unknown**, not estimated from the chassis.
- The [official product photograph](https://static.roland.com/products/gt-1000core/images/gt-1000core_top_gal.jpg) was successfully opened and visually inspected: **monochrome-style readout**. Colored footswitch LEDs are not a color graphical display. The manufacturer does not separately state the screen's color depth; this is visual evidence, not a panel-datasheet claim.
- **Touchscreen: unverified/no touch interface documented**. Color/monochrome appearance and knob navigation alone are not definitive touch-hardware specifications.

The [manufacturer product/specification page](https://www.boss.info/global/products/gt-1000core/) also confirms **7 knobs,3 footswitches,5 buttons**,24 simultaneous blocks,185 effect types,250 factory+250 user locations and16 IR slots. It lists system program2.02 and Tone Studio4.1.1; this is a publication observation, not the owner's installed version. Complete current category counts and synth/acoustic algorithms remain open.

### GT-1000CORE — original USD and technical provenance

Research date **2026-10-14**. Technical sources are the linked manufacturer's specifications, including current firmware-dependent figures. **Partial records**: `?` means unverified. Dimensions are **W × D × H in mm**; `/max` gives the maximum height with the pedal raised. `K/F/B` = rotary controls / footswitches / separate buttons, excluding the expression toe switch and rear power/mode switches. Price columns are **USD US-market listed/asking observations**, not statistically estimated fair values; see sources below.

Model totals have different inclusion rules. ME-90's selectable alternatives are not all simultaneously present on its panel. A missing NAM claim is not converted into a definitive “No” without model/firmware documentation.

Price retrieval date: **2026-10-14**. Indexed older listings are explicitly less reliable than current, purchasable offers. No guarantee of stock, local Indonesian pricing, delivery cost or completed-sale value.

Checked **2026-10-15**. Existing model in [BOSS comparison](BOSS.md)<!-- historical-link:boss-014 -->, not an additional catalogue entry.

### Display and directly rechecked specifications

The [manufacturer page](https://www.boss.info/global/products/gx-10/) explicitly specifies **one color graphic LCD,480×272 pixels, with touchscreen**. **Diagonal remains unknown**: a numeric size was not located in the checked primary text and is not inferred from the chassis or retailer wording. The [manufacturer getting-started article](https://articles.boss.info/getting-started-with-the-boss-gx-10/) confirms actual finger-based editing and says the display is the same design introduced on GX-100.

The current product specification confirms **6 knobs,3 footswitches,5 buttons**, plus expression/toe and power separately;15 assignable blocks;32 amps split23 guitar/9 bass;170 effects. Windows/macOS editing and **iOS/Android editing with optional BT-DUAL** remain distinct from ordinary USB mobile recording. This scoped review does not independently certify the complete effect-category inventory or every earlier power/loading field.

Checked **2026-10-16**. These rows update the dated facts above. The retained source body and historical rows preserve all earlier observations.

<!-- comparison evidence begins -->

| Required field | Current evidence and limits |
|---|---|
| Expression | Built-in pedal with toe switch; one shared CTL 2,3/EXP 2 socket accepts external expression or footswitches. [GX10-P] |
| K/F/B | **6 / 3 / 5**; power and expression toe switches counted separately. [GX10-P] |
| Display | **480×272 color touchscreen**; published diagonal remains unknown. [GX10-P] |
| Dimensions / weight / size | **300×183×74 mm**, maximum height 96 mm; **2.2 kg**. Medium under the repository's width/depth filter. [GX10-P] |
| Simultaneous processing / category choices | Product specification: **15+3 utilities**. HTML manual describes 15 effects/devices including utilities; counting wording differs. DSP-limited; maximum nine of the same effect, two amps and one each looper, divider/mixer and send/return. 32 amps (23 guitar/9 bass),170 effects retained; complete per-category selectable totals unresolved. [GX10-CHAIN], [GX10-DSP] |
| Factory / user / total memory | **99 preset +198 user =297** locations. [GX10-P] |
| Synth / acoustic | **AC.GUITAR SIMULATOR** and **AC RESONANCE** explicitly listed. Sitar Simulator, Humanizer and Ring Modulator alone do not establish a general synth engine. [GX10-DSP] |
| IR / NAM / capture | **16 user IR slots**; WAV at **44.1/48/96 kHz,16/24-bit linear**. Tone Studio converts IRs before loading USER-1–16; this is not NAM conversion. Native NAM, converted NAM and proprietary capture imports unestablished. [GX10-IR] |
| Audio / control I/O | Mono quarter-inch input; two quarter-inch outputs; mono SEND/RETURN; stereo quarter-inch PHONES; CTL/EXP; USB-C; dedicated BT-DUAL slot; DC. [GX10-P] |
| Power / battery / USB power | Included **PSB-1U**, **600 mA draw**. Voltage/polarity, battery and USB-powered operation remain unverified. [GX10-P] |
| Editor / USB / platforms | Tone Studio Windows/macOS; mobile editing needs optional BT-DUAL. USB audio/MIDI; VENDOR requires dedicated computer driver, GENERIC uses iOS standard functionality with limits. Dry recording and DAW-to-effects reamp; loopback. Direct monitor resets ON at power-up and is bypassed by loopback. Exact USB channel/bit-depth and Android USB compatibility unresolved. Firmware1.06, editor1.0.2, drivers1.0.0 listed, not installed-version proof. [GX10-USB], [GX10-PC], [GX10-P] |
| Looper / routing / live use | **38 s mono /19 s stereo**, overdubbing. Insert LOOP and assign switches; LOOP CTL cycles record/play/dub, double press stops, long press erases. External switches can separately stop/clear. Serial/parallel placement; no drums or scenes claim established. [GX10-LOOP], [GX10-CHAIN] |

## GT-1

Consolidated and refreshed **2026-10-16**; partial technical verification. Original dates, prices and limitations remain below. Historical next-model notes are provenance; use research-state.json for the current queue.

<!-- BOSS-GT-1 source begins -->

Checked **2026-10-15**. Existing [BOSS comparison](BOSS.md)<!-- historical-link:boss-018 --> model; this finding adds no inventory count.

### Display

The [manufacturer specification](https://www.boss.info/global/products/gt-1/) identifies a **132×32-dot backlit graphic LCD**. The [official front photograph](https://static.roland.com/assets/images/products/main/gt-1_main.jpg) successfully rendered and shows a **monochrome readout**; blue footswitch trim does not make it a color screen. **Diagonal is not published in the inspected source**. Explicit touchscreen hardware specification remains unverified/no touch interface documented; it is not inferred from the button layout.

The same primary specification rechecks **3 knobs,3 footswitches,12 buttons**,99 user+99 factory presets,108 effect types and32-second looper. It lists fourAA batteries or a separately sold PSA adapter, not USB bus power. These scoped checks preserve the earlier record rather than re-certifying every algorithm.

### IDR evidence

| Condition / channel | Amount | Source and limitation |
|---|---:|---|
| Shopee retail | **Rp4.000.000** | [Eleven Music,shop12356205/item23161745476](https://shopee.co.id/Boss-GT-1-Guitar-Multi-Effects-Pedal-BOSS-GT1-Original-i.12356205.23161745476); indexed hardware offer, checkout/condition detail unverified |
| Separate retail | **Rp4.000.000** | [CH MUSIC,shop195817632/item22261754122](https://shopee.co.id/BOSS-GT-1-Guitar-Effects-Processor-Efek-Pedal-Gitar-BOSS-GT1-i.195817632.22261754122) |
| Used, seller says like new | **Rp2.500.000 — old profile-listed ask** | [Carousell seller profile](https://id.carousell.com/u/doyox666/) explicitly lists GT-1,item1317082740,condition Seperti baru; about2-year-old listing. Exact item reopening failed, so not confirmed active |
| Historical retail | **Rp3.530.000 — SOLD OUT** | [GH MUSIC,shop71552895/item23582874470](https://shopee.co.id/BOSS-GT-1-Guitar-Effects-Processor-Efek-Pedal-Gitar-Digital-GT1-i.71552895.23582874470) |

An adapter bundle is a different offer from a bare processor. Mixed GT-1/GX-1 titles and an OLX result with unverified individual condition were not used as per-model price bounds. The used profile is evidence of an asking price, not a completed sale or guaranteed purchasable unit. Seller authenticity, shipping, warranty, selected accessories and firmware remain unverified.

Old USD offers remain historical provenance. Remaining: manufacturer-published diagonal, explicit touch specification and fuller technical/price verification. **Next existing model: GX-1.**

### GT-1 — current comparison evidence

Checked **2026-10-16**. [Manufacturer page](https://www.boss.info/global/products/gt-1/) marks discontinued; this does not establish every seller lacks old stock.

<!-- comparison evidence begins -->

| Required field | Current evidence and limits |
|---|---|
| Expression | Built-in pedal and toe switch; one shared CTL2,3/EXP2 socket for external expression or switches. [GT1-M] |
| K/F/B | **3 /3 /12**; expression toe separate. Input insertion switches power on. [GT1-P], [GT1-M] |
| Display | **132×32 backlit graphic LCD**; monochrome appearance from previously inspected official photograph. Published diagonal and explicit touch specification remain unverified. [GT1-P] |
| Dimensions / weight / size | **305×152×56 mm**, maximum height74 mm; **1.3 kg including batteries**. Large under repository width filter. [GT1-P] |
| Simultaneous processing / category choices | FX1,OD/DS,PREAMP,FX2,DELAY,REVERB plus pedal/utilities; **108 types**, not108 simultaneous blocks. FX1 and FX2 can select the same effect. Parameter guide p6 gives **23 top-level FX1/FX2 choices each**: Compressor,Limiter,T.Wah,GraphicEQ,ParaEQ,ToneModify,GuitarSim,Ac.GtrSim,SlowGear,Octave,PitchShifter,Harmonist,Overtone,Feedbacker,Ac.Processor,Phaser,Flanger,Tremolo,Rotary,Uni-V,Vibrato,Chorus,SubDelay. **OD/DS22; PREAMP27; DELAY7; REVERB-slot8; PEDAL FX7**, counted from guide pp11–15. Nested types: **Compressor8,Limiter3,ToneModify8,GuitarSimulator8,Ac.Processor4,Phaser4**. Subtypes are not independent blocks; manufacturer-stated simultaneous ceiling and108 counting basis remain unresolved. [GT1-P], [GT1-G] |
| Factory / user / total memory | **99 preset +99 user =198**. [GT1-P] |
| Synth / acoustic | **AC.GUITAR SIMULATOR**, pickup/body GUITAR SIMULATOR and **AC.PROCESSOR** for electro-acoustic pickups. OVERTONE adds harmonics; this does not prove a general synth engine. [GT1-G] |
| IR / NAM / capture | No third-party IR loader, native NAM, converted NAM or proprietary capture import established. Factory speaker simulations are distinct from user IR import. |
| Audio / control I/O | Mono quarter-inch input; stereo quarter-inch outputs;3.5 mm stereo AUX and PHONES; shared TRS CTL/EXP;USB-B;DC. No loop or physical MIDI connector listed. [GT1-P] |
| Power / battery / USB power | Four alkaline AA or separately sold PSA adapter; **200 mA draw**, about **7 h** alkaline runtime. Owner manual p2 recommends keeping batteries installed for continued performance if the adapter disconnects; BATTERY LOW warning is provided. DC voltage/polarity and USB-powered operation remain unverified. [GT1-P], [GT1-M] |
| Editor / USB / platforms | Tone Studio Windows/macOS; dedicated USB driver required for computer audio. Owner manual p8 confirms patch and system-setting backup/restore, patch naming and ordering; USB2.0 cable specified. USB diagram sends effected audio to computer and mixes computer return after the chain. **USB MIX LEVEL and EFFECT OUT LEVEL each0–200%**; direct monitor is unsaved and resets ON at power-up. With direct monitor OFF, only USB return is heard unless the computer monitors the input. USB reamp is not established by that diagram. **macOS Tone Studio1.0.2 (March2018)** requires the matching USB driver. Its fixes cover incorrect parameter transfers, ASSIGN TARGET MIN/MAX input/display and unavailable delay parameters shown editable. Version1.0.1 added Sierra10.12 support; BOSS’s matrix updated2026-09-16 lists GT-1 **Hardware & Driver and Exclusive Application: Yes(Download) for macOS15 Sequoia and26 Tahoe; Under Testing for27 Golden Gate**, checked2026-10-17. Driver listings for newer macOS/Windows do not independently prove editor compatibility. Mobile editor, channels and bit depth unresolved. [GT1-M], [GT1-G], [GT1-EDITOR], [GT1-MAC] |
| Looper / routing / live use | **32 s mono**, overdub; recordings lost when looper or power is switched off. Looper overrides stored CTL1 assignments. Effect order editable; detailed routing, drums/scenes unestablished. **System1.09 (Dec2021) changes version management only, with specifications unchanged. 1.08 fixes tuner pedal combinations, held-pedal FX TYPE list behavior and Tone Studio CLEAR for CTL/ASSIGN. 1.07 fixes delayed pitch-shifted sound after patch changes.** Installed version unknown. [GT1-M], [GT1-G], [GT1-FW] |

[GT1-MAC]: https://www.boss.info/global/support/support_news/25090811m1/
[GT1-FW]: https://www.boss.info/nl/support/by_product/gt-1/updates_drivers/076a30a8-a319-4891-94e4-ef0bb08451e5/

Firmware follow-up2026-10-17: global detail repeatedly timed out; manufacturer Dutch page’s English history supplies the evidence above. Its final instruction still says verify1.07 despite1.09 heading/archive: retained as a documentation inconsistency, not a firmware downgrade instruction. No update performed or downloaded. Current macOS matrix supersedes the earlier unresolved compatibility status for its explicitly listed versions only.

GT-1 parameter follow-up checked2026-10-17: own Parameter Guide/Sound List ©2016, p6 type enumeration and p25 USB settings. Counts describe selector choices, not a newly reconciled108-type total. The PDF was read through the web reader; no file downloaded or visual inspection claimed.

### GT-1 — selector-count qualifications

Checked2026-10-17 against [own parameter guide][GT1-G], pp11–15. Delay choices: Standard,Pan,Reverse,Analog,Tape,Modulate,TeraEcho. Reverb slot: Ambience,Room,Hall1,Hall2,Plate,Spring,Modulate,**Delay**. Pedal: CryWah,VoWah,FatWah,LightWah,7StrWah,ResoWah,PedalBend. PedalBend is single-note,±24semitones; chords unsupported. Main delay reaches2000ms,FX SubDelay1000ms,reverb-slot Delay650ms. A following mono effect/COSM amp collapses stereo delay. Counts represent alternatives, not extra concurrent blocks; nested compressor/limiter/etc. types still need separate accounting.

### GT-1 — nested selector evidence

Checked2026-10-17, [own guide][GT1-G] pp6–10: Compressor8 (BOSS,Hi-Band,Light,D-Comp,Orange,Fat,Mild,Stereo); Limiter3 (BOSS,Rack160D,VtgRackU); ToneModify8 includes Resonator1/2/3 separately; GuitarSimulator8 pickup/body transformations; AcousticProcessor4 (Small,Medium,Bright,Power); Phaser4 (4/8/12Stage,BiPhase). T.Wah has LPF/BPF modes and Up/Down response; Chorus has Mono/Stereo1/Stereo2; SubDelay has Mono/Pan. Mode switches, continuous parameters and nested types must not be indiscriminately summed with top-level effects. Harmonist requires single-note playing; this restriction is not generalized to every pitch effect.

GT-1 owner-manual follow-up2026-10-17: own ©2016 manual pp2/8 read through web text. Battery fallback and backup scope are explicit; no USB bus-power statement was located. Absence of that statement is not proof USB power is impossible. No software, manual file or firmware downloaded; no device configuration changed.

## GX-1

Consolidated and refreshed **2026-10-16**; partial verification. This is GX-1, not GX-1B. Full original facts and observation dates remain below. Historical next-model notes are provenance; current work is in research-state.json.

<!-- BOSS-GX-1 source begins -->

Checked **2026-10-15**. Existing model in [BOSS comparison](BOSS.md)<!-- historical-link:boss-022 -->; not an additional catalogue entry and not GX-1B.

### Display and directly rechecked hardware

The [manufacturer specification](https://www.boss.info/global/products/gx-1/) confirms **one color graphic LCD,320×240 pixels**. A published diagonal was not located. **Touchscreen remains unverified/no touch interface documented**; references to touching the separate effect-category buttons do not prove touchscreen hardware.

The enumerated controls confirm **3 knobs,3 main footswitches,14 separate buttons**, plus expression/toe switching. There are99 user+99 factory memories and146 effect types. Eight simultaneous effects plus volume/noise suppression are distinct from the total model library.

Power is explicitly **fourAA batteries,PSA adapter or USB bus**, with250mA DC/battery and400mA USB draw. The official [GX-1 support page](https://www.boss.info/global/products/gx-1/support/) confirms Windows/macOS/iOS/Android Tone Studio, built-in Bluetooth and WAV IR loading. Those checks do not complete the remaining algorithm/IR-capacity fields.

Dry output, DAW-to-effects input and loopback documented. Direct monitor resets ON at power-up. USB channels/bit depth and Android USB compatibility unresolved. [GX1-P], [GX1-USB] |

## ME-90

Consolidated and refreshed **2026-10-16**; partial verification. This is the guitar ME-90, not ME-90B. Original dates, sources and price qualifications remain below. Historical next-model notes are provenance; use research-state.json for current work.

<!-- BOSS-ME-90 source begins -->

Checked **2026-10-15**. Updates an existing [BOSS comparison](BOSS.md)<!-- historical-link:boss-026 --> record; not another model.

### Display and controls

The [manufacturer specification](https://www.boss.info/global/products/me-90/) explicitly identifies a **two-character, seven-segment LED display**, not a graphical LCD. Graphical-screen diagonal/color classification is **N/A**; physical readout dimensions/color are not published in the checked specification. **No touchscreen interface** is documented. Multicolored footswitch status LEDs must not be described as a color graphical display.

The control list rechecks **25 knobs,8 main footswitches,4 buttons**, plus expression toe, rear power and output-mode switches separately. The page confirms36 factory+36 user presets,97 effect types,11 onboard amps,3 user IR slots and a38-second mono looper. Windows/macOS Tone Studio and IR loader remain separate applications; iOS/Android editing needs optional BT-DUAL. This is a scoped review, not a complete current algorithm-list transcription.

### Separate IDR evidence

| Channel / condition | Amount | Exact evidence and qualification |
|---|---:|---|
| Shopee retail, Eleven Music | **Rp7.450.000** | [shop12356205/item25352150117](https://shopee.co.id/Boss-ME-90-Guitar-Multi-Effects-Pedal-BOSS-ME90-Effect-Gitar-Original-i.12356205.25352150117); indexed hardware ask, detailed condition/checkout not verified |
| Indonesian retailer Swee Lee | **Rp7.633.000** | [Retail page](https://www.sweelee.co.id/en/products/boss-me-90-guitar-multi-effects-pedal), opened and displays price, tax inclusion and in-store/online availability; delivery terms remain subject to checkout |
| Shopee voucher-dependent | **Rp7.590.000 WITH VOUCHER** | [Gallery Music Bekasi shop760002726/item24507018815](https://shopee.co.id/Boss-ME-90-Guitar-Multiple-Effect-ME90-Efek-Gitar-i.760002726.24507018815); not an unconditional bare-unit amount |
| Historical retail | **Rp8.980.000 — SOLD OUT** | [Melodia/Roland Store JKT shop30194339/item22780355508](https://shopee.co.id/Boss-ME-90-Guitar-Multi-effects-Pedal-i.30194339.22780355508) |
| Used | **Unknown verified individual quote** | OLX search pages expose Rp5.300.000 ME-90 and Rp4.500.000 with PSA adapter, but individual ads/condition could not be opened; retained below as discovery only |

Used-price leads: [OLX Studio/Panggung search](https://www.olx.co.id/bekasi-kota_g4000020/alat-musik-aksesoris_c217/q-boss?filter=type_eq_hobi-olahraga-alat-alat-musik-studio-panggung), [Bogor effects search](https://www.olx.co.id/bogor-kota_g4000021/alat-musik-aksesoris_c217/q-efek-bass). A category filter containing Bekas is not sufficient to assign an independently verified condition to an unopened individual ad. Neither lead is promoted to a confirmed active used lower bound.

The manufacturer includes batteries, not the optional PSA adapter. A retailer [explicitly lists the adapter as excluded](https://www.blibli.com/p/boss-me-90-guitar-multi-effects-pedal/ps--MEM-70158-00082). Another Shopee item mixes four variations and sold-out states; its whole range is not treated as the processor's market price. Seller authenticity, accessories, region and delivered cost remain unverified unless specifically stated.

Official product-image attempts failed; the segmented-display classification comes from the explicit specification, not an uninspected photo. Original USD observations remain historical. **Next existing-model review: GX-100.**

## GX-100

Consolidated and refreshed **2026-10-16**; partial verification. Full original observations, dates and qualifications remain below. Historical next-model notes are provenance; current queue is in research-state.json.

<!-- BOSS-GX-100 source begins -->

Checked **2026-10-15**. Existing [BOSS comparison](BOSS.md)<!-- historical-link:boss-030 --> record, not another model.

### Display and firmware-sensitive correction

The [manufacturer specification](https://www.boss.info/global/products/gx-100/) confirms a **color graphic LCD,480×272 pixels, with touchscreen**. A numeric diagonal was not found in the checked primary source. Retailers/reviews commonly claim4.3 inches, but this pass does not promote that to manufacturer-verified diagonal evidence.

The official page now advertises **32 AIRD amps**, rather than the launch23. Its version2.0 section explains three added guitar and six added bass amps, seven new effects and eight new factory presets. Current specification still lists170 effect types and100 preset+200 user memories; overlapping marketing counts are not summed. The control list rechecks **6 knobs,8 footswitches,7 buttons**, plus expression toe/power and other rear switches separately.

Windows/macOS Tone Studio now imports speaker IRs without a separate loader application. iOS/Android wireless editing continues to require optional BT-DUAL. Full category subtotals, exact IR capacity and remaining synth/acoustic algorithms are not independently re-certified in this scoped pass.

## GT-1000

Consolidated and refreshed **2026-10-16**; partial verification. Full-size GT-1000 is distinct from GT-1000CORE. Original observations and historical next-model notes remain below as provenance; current queue is in research-state.json.

Checked **2026-10-15**. Existing [BOSS comparison](BOSS.md)<!-- historical-link:boss-034 --> model; separate from GT-1000CORE.

### Display and scoped technical checks

The [manufacturer specification](https://www.boss.info/global/products/gt-1000/) establishes **one backlit graphic LCD,512×160 pixels**. It does not provide a diagonal or explicit color-depth/touch specification in the inspected text. Attempts to open the official front/top photos failed, so this pass does **not** claim a successful visual monochrome/touch verification. The color and touch fields retain an explicit evidence gap rather than borrowing GT-1000CORE's screen.

The current page confirms **7 rotary controls,10 main footswitches,5 separate buttons**. Its feature text explicitly identifies the expression **toe switch**, which was not stated in the old hardware row. Current totals are185 effect types and250 factory+250 user memories; older145-type retailer copy is not the current source of truth. The built-in Bluetooth editor supports iOS/Android, alongside Windows/macOS Tone Studio; the product does not require BT-DUAL.

These checks do not finish current category subtotals, exact synth/acoustic algorithm names, USB-bus-power verification or every earlier field. No launch-era effect count is silently promoted to current firmware.

## GM-800

Consolidated and refreshed **2026-10-16**; partial verification. This processor qualifies through independent MFX architecture; it requires specialized input hardware for guitar/bass playing. No excluded specialist model is added.

Technical research snapshot: **2026-10-14**. All records remain partial; `?` means unverified, not No. This reclassification preserves earlier sources and uncertainties; it is not a fresh technical verification. Dimensions are W×D×H in millimetres; cables and pedal travel require additional clearance.

<!-- BOSS-GM-800 source begins -->

Included as a multi-instrument synthesizer with multiple independent MFX stages, not merely because it contains multiple synth sounds. Keep its specialized pickup requirements visible rather than treating it as a conventional analog-input guitar processor.

| Field | Preserved comparison evidence |
|---|---|
| Built-in / external expression | No built-in pedal; **2 CTL/EXP sockets**, up to 4 external switches |
| Rotary / footswitch / separate buttons | **6 / 4 / 10** |
| Dimensions / weight / size | **246×134×63 mm**, H65 with feet; **1.2 kg** excluding adapter; medium |
| Screen size / color / touchscreen | **? / ? / ?**; original display review still pending |
| Used IDR / new IDR | **? / ?**; no verified price observation retained for this model |
| Architecture | **4 Tone parts + 1 Rhythm part**; 1200+ tones, 70+ drum kits |
| Effect categories / choices | Each part has MFX with **93 types** and EQ; scene has **2 MFX**, each 93 types, plus reverb **7** and chorus/delay **9** types |
| Presets / memory | **150 editable Scenes, 100 factory-filled**; separate import-tone memory |
| Synth / acoustic | ZEN-Core instrument synth with instrument-style tones; exact acoustic-simulation distinction remains to be verified |
| IR / NAM / proprietary content | Roland Cloud expansions are proprietary tone content; user IR and NAM loading not established |
| Connections | **Serial GK IN/OUT**; 2×¼″ audio outputs, L shared with phones; 2×CTL/EXP; DIN MIDI IN/OUT; USB-B computer; USB-memory port; DC |
| Power / USB power | Supplied **PSB-1U adapter; 840 mA**; battery/USB bus power not listed |
| Configuration / USB functions | BOSS Tone Studio **Windows/macOS**; USB audio/MIDI, guitar-to-MIDI and re-synthesis |
| Main limitation | GK-5/GK-5B or compatible conversion required for guitar input; Serial GK is not an ordinary analog guitar jack |

The Serial GK connection uses the manufacturer's **BGK cable** and an appropriate divided pickup/interface. Older 13-pin GK equipment requires the appropriate **GKC-AD** conversion; a standard guitar cable is not a substitute. MIDI keyboards/controllers can also drive the sound engine. Primary source: [BOSS GM-800](https://www.boss.info/global/products/gm-800/).
<!-- BOSS-GM-800 source ends -->

[Complete original source](BOSS.md#gm-800)<!-- historical-link:boss-038 --> retained unchanged; no source deleted.

### GM-800 — current comparison evidence

Checked **2026-10-16**. Original2026-10-14 facts above remain preserved; firmware-specific additions below are separate.

<!-- comparison evidence begins -->

| Required field | Current evidence and limits |
|---|---|
| Expression | No built-in expression or toe switch. **Two shared CTL/EXP sockets**, up to four external switches or two expression pedals. [GM800-P] |
| K/F/B | **6 /4 /10** retained from manufacturer inventory: OUTPUT LEVEL,SELECT and1–4 knobs; UP/DOWN,CTL1/2 footswitches;five PART,EXIT,WRITE,MENU,two PAGE buttons. Reference manual eng02 labels1–4 as buttons in prose, while startup guide/product list identifies knobs; retain that terminology discrepancy. [GM800-P], [GM800-M] |
| Display | **256×80 graphic LCD**. Published diagonal,color depth and touchscreen specification unverified. [GM800-P] |
| Dimensions / weight / size | **246×134×63 mm**,65 mm with rubber feet; **1.2 kg excluding adapter**. Medium. [GM800-P] |
| Simultaneous processing / category choices | **4 Tone parts +1 Rhythm part**. Each has independent MFX/EQ; scene adds2 MFX plus chorus/delay,reverb;system Master Comp/EQ. Original **93 MFX choices each,7 reverb,9 chorus/delay** retained; not simultaneous93 effects.1200+ tones,70+ drum kits;current firmware category subtotals remain unchecked. [GM800-P] |
| Factory / user / total memory | **150 editable Scenes,100 factory-filled** retained,not150+100. Separate imported-tone memory capacity remains unchecked. |
| Synth / acoustic | ZEN-Core instrument synthesizer for guitar/bass or MIDI control. Acoustic instrument tones are not proof of an ordinary analog guitar acoustic-simulation path. **GK-5/GK-5B and BGK cable**,or appropriate compatible divided-pickup conversion;13-pin GK requires GKC-AD. No ordinary analog instrument input. [GM800-P] |
| IR / NAM / capture | Proprietary imported tones: **SVZ**,Sound Packs **SDZ**,Wave Expansions **EXZ**,via USB flash drive. These are not IR,NAM or amp-capture files. User IR,native NAM,converted NAM and proprietary amp-capture import unestablished. [GM800-M] |
| Audio / control I/O | Serial GK IN/OUT (TRS shape,**BGK cable only**);two quarter-inch outputs,L shared with stereo phones;two TRS CTL/EXP;DIN MIDI IN/OUT;USB-B COMPUTER;USB MEMORY;DC. No conventional analog guitar or mic input listed. [GM800-P] |
| Power / battery / USB power | Included **PSB-1U**, **840 mA draw**. DC insertion/removal switches power. Voltage/polarity,battery and USB-powered operation unverified. [GM800-P], [GM800-M] |
| Editor / USB / platforms | Tone Studio **Windows/macOS**;computer USB audio/MIDI and re-synthesis. Dedicated USB driver required. USB flash backup includes scenes,imported tones and system settings;this differs from computer audio or editing. Mobile editor,exact USB channels/rates/bitdepth unresolved. [GM800-P], [GM800-M] |
| Routing / scenes / drums / live use | Scenes control four Tone parts and Rhythm/drum part;not a verified drum-machine sequencer or looper. Firmware **1.10 May2024** adds GK5-KIT support including7-string G7 and normal-pickup OUTPUT MODE; **1.20 Jan2025** adds GK Audio and PART HOLD assignment; **1.50 July2026** adds responsiveness-focused TRACKING MODE. Matching Tone Studio updates required;not installed-version proof. Detailed GK Audio/routing/looper limits still open. [GM800-U] |

## GT-100

Checked **2026-10-16**. Guitar COSM multi-effects, distinct from GT-1000. Partial first pass; the original manual URL was found but web extraction failed because its PDF exceeded the fetch size limit. No manual file was downloaded.

| Field | Model-specific evidence / unresolved fields |
|---|---|
| Expression | Built-in treadle; external SUB CTL1,2/SUB EXP shared socket. Toe operation requires manual verification. [Product][GT100-P] |
| K/F/B | Eight parameter knobs advertised; total rotary count including output level,independent footswitch/button counts require panel verification. |
| Display | Two backlit132×64 LCDs; diagonal,color/monochrome and touch remain unverified. |
| Dimensions / weight | 542×271×80mm; maximum height102mm;4.8kg. |
| Simultaneous processing / categories | Dual A/B paths; numerical simultaneous-block ceiling unverified. Product lists COMP,PREAMP,OD/DS,EQ,FX1/2,DELAY,CHORUS,REVERB,PEDAL FX,NS1/2,ACCEL FX. Current per-category totals need parameter-guide reconciliation. |
| Factory / user / total memory | 200 preset+200 user=400 locations. |
| Synth / acoustic | WAVE SYNTH,AC.GUITAR SIM and AC.PROCESSOR listed; simulation and acoustic processing are separate functions. |
| IR / native NAM / converted NAM / captures | Import/capture support unverified; do not infer from COSM modeling or USB. |
| Audio / control I/O | Quarter-inch input,L/mono+R outputs,stereo quarter-inch phones,send/return,amp control,TRS external control;stereo mini AUX;USB,DIN MIDI in/out,DC input. |
| Power / battery / USB power | 9V DC,600mA; PSB-1U adapter. Polarity,battery/USB operation unresolved. |
| Editor / drivers | BOSS TONE STUDIO Mac/Windows requires system2.0. Product support lists system2.12,editor1.1.3 and separate Windows10/11/macOS audio drivers; exact OS requirements/current updater procedure not yet verified. |
| Looper / scenes / routing | A/B blending/switching; playing strength/frequency switching. Accel controls multiple parameters. Looper duration,drums,scene behavior and USB channel count require manual review. |

Technical table source: [BOSS product specifications and version-2 notes][GT100-P]. **Firmware limits:** A-DIST is in OD/DS and FX2 SUB OD/DS; OVERTONE and TERA ECHO are FX2-only. Do not treat the FX1/FX2 combined list as two identical freely interchangeable libraries.

### ME-20B — pedal calibration and live tuner, 2026-10-18

| Field | Original-manual detail / qualification | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| Expression / calibration | In Manual mode, hold EDIT/EXIT and press pedal3; calibrate heel then toe with WRITE. The manual says the display shows “1–5”, but the next step explicitly allows **1–9 sensitivity values**, with5 factory default and lower values needing less switch force. Preserve this internal range inconsistency; no physical calibration was performed. | [Original manual][M20B-M] p30 |
| Tuner / bypass | Press number pedals1+2 together to enter; either that combination or EDIT/EXIT exits. The display covers lowB through highC string references as well as chromatic note names. This does not establish synthesis polyphony or added effect slots. | [Original manual][M20B-M] pp14–15 |

AC.PROCESSOR separately reshapes electro-acoustic pickup sound; Small/Medium/Bright/Power types. This supersedes unverified synth/acoustic status. | [Manual pp106–107,112][G10-R]; [Indexed official p106][G10-M]; [Acoustic-use Q&A][G10-A] |

Windows10 driver1.0.0 and macOS10.13/10.14 driver1.0.3 listed; no current-OS blanket claim. System Program1.02 is an updater, not an editor. | [Support listing][G10-F] |
| Firmware / live restrictions | System Program **1.02** fixes patch-change sound loss/abnormal noise, GRAPHIC EQ acting despite being off, and recalled effect states becoming inconsistent during rapid patch changes when Smooth mode or PHRASE LOOP is active. Also fixes FX CHAIN meter cursor tracking; improves chorus-mode transitions and category-keyword refresh. These are version-specific fixes, not proof of universal gapless switching. | [Japanese release notes][G10-U] |
| Firmware / memory maintenance | Update preserves user memory; BOSS recommends Librarian or bulk-dump backup. Transfer uses MIDI or USB with the dedicated driver and a SysEx-capable SMF utility; UpdSMF is an update transport, not a patch editor. | [Japanese release notes][G10-U] |
| Editor / Windows restrictions | Librarian1.00 manages user-patch collections; official page lists Windows XP/Vista/7/8/8.1/10. Its reference to an Editor does not identify or verify a separate full editor. Driver1.0.0 supports Windows10 32/64-bit, excludes Mobile, virtual Windows and Windows on Mac; USB3 ports must support USB2. Roland support updated2025-05-19 explicitly says **Windows11 unsupported**. This concerns the GT-10 driver, not proof every standard-audio mode fails. | [Librarian][G10-L]; [Driver][G10-D]; [Windows support][G10-T] |
| USB audio / recording modes | Standard mode uses the OS driver but **cannot use USB MIDI**; MIDI requires Advanced mode and the dedicated driver. Changing driver mode requires restart. Official support specifies a USB A–B cable, establishing USB-B on the unit. Direct Monitor controls GT-10 sound at OUTPUT/PHONES, resets On at power-up and can be controlled by ASIO2 software with the special driver. Off requires computer monitoring to hear the guitar. | [Indexed manual pp91,93][G10-M]; [Windows support][G10-T] |
| External control / assignments | Eight ASSIGN1–8 mappings. EXP1,EXP2,EXPSW,CTL1–4 can independently follow patch or system settings; system overrides ignore rather than erase patch settings. Switch behavior supports toggle/momentary. MIDI CC, input level, internal timed pedal and tempo-synced wave pedal are control sources. Foot volume is movable in-chain, distinct from post-effects overall volume. | [Workshop pp3–5,9–12][G10-W] |
| Looper / drums / live | Manufacturer Q&A confirms **38s mono /19s stereo**, overdubbing and **no saving the recorded performance**. This supports the workshop38s figure; Finnish product40s remains a conflicting regional claim. BANK pedals operate basic looping; additional assignments extend control. Manual p60 allows retaining bank switching by assigning loop control to CTL1–4; Clear Only erases immediately, while Mute/Clear first mutes and a second press erases. Drums/scenes/trails unverified. | [Q&A][G10-Q]; [Workshop pp2,5][G10-W]; [Regional product][G10-F]; [Indexed manual p60][G10-M] |

Guitar multi-effects; distinct from GT-6/GT-10. Checked2026-10-17; partial. Bass suitability and ownership unestablished.

| Expression | Built-in expression with **firm-press toe switch**. Shared SUB EXP/SUB CTL1,2 accepts external expression (EV-5) or footswitch (FS-6/FS-5U). External toe behavior unverified. | [Product][G8-P]; [Indexed manual pp11–12][G8-M] |
| K/F/B | **17 /7 /31**, text-derived: fifteen tone knobs +PATCH/VALUE +rear OUTPUT LEVEL; four numbered +two BANK +CTL footswitches. Buttons: seven preamp/drive, twelve delay-through-MASTER, twelve navigation/system/assign/manual. Toe and rear power additional. Visual corroboration pending. | [Indexed manual pp9–12,17][G8-M] |
| Display | Backlit16-character×2-line LCD; two-character seven-segment LED. Diagonal/colour/touch unverified. | [Product][G8-P] |
| Dimensions / mass | 515W×261D×75H mm;4.8kg. Large. | [Product][G8-P] |
| Processing / categories | **13 simultaneous blocks**, freely ordered; **44 effect categories**, **46 COSM amps**, each with Solo variation. Detailed category totals/counting basis pending. | [Product][G8-P] |
| FX category choices | **FX-1:22 common choices; FX-2:the same22 +11 additional =33**. FX-2-only: Harmonist, Pitch Shifter, Pedal Bend, Octave, Rotary,2×2 Chorus, Auto Riff, Guitar Synth, Acoustic Processor, Sound Hold, Sub Delay. Same common type may occupy both slots. Choice counts, not simultaneous blocks. | [Manual p34][G8-R]; [Indexed official p43][G8-M] |
| Category choices / types | OD/DS30 fixed +3 Custom; DELAY12 including Hold; CHORUS3 modes; REVERB7; COMP2 modes; WAH6 fixed +3 Custom. Selectable alternatives, not additional concurrent blocks. | [Manual pp29–34][G8-R] |
| Delay / live restrictions | Normal delay0–1800ms; dual delays each0–900ms. Immediate playing after dual-delay patch changes can lose the intended initial effect. Switching Modulate↔Hold clears held sound and disables active Feedbacker OSC, Auto Riff Hold and Sound Hold. | [Manual p30][G8-R] |
| Memory / shared custom definitions | Three Custom amp,drive,wah definitions and two Custom speakers are shared across patches; editing one changes every patch referencing it. They are not independent copies in each patch. | [Manual pp63–66][G8-R] |
| MIDI software / backup | SysEx Bulk Dump/Load supports System, Quick, user-patch range1-1–35-4 and Temp. System includes Harmonist scales, Auto Riff phrases and custom definitions; patch-only backup is narrower. Sender/receiver Device IDs must match. External MIDI clock can take over Master BPM; Internal restores local tempo. No full-editor platform support inferred. | [Manual pp80–83][G8-R] |
| Factory / user memory | **340 total:140 user+200 preset**. User banks1–35; read-only preset banks36–85; four patches/bank. Edited presets save into user slots. Write required to retain knob edits. Initial user-bank fill pending. | [Product][G8-P]; [Indexed manual pp16–17][G8-M] |
| Synth / acoustic | Wave Synth plus **FX-2 Guitar Synth**, which requires single notes, muted unused strings and clear attacks. **GS acoustic simulation yes**: single-coil/humbucker/piezo-to-acoustic choices; separate FX-2 Acoustic Processor. Harmonist also requires single-note input. | [Product][G8-P]; [Indexed manual pp43,47][G8-M]; [Manual transcript p36][G8-R] |
| I/O | INPUT;L/MONO/R OUTPUT;PHONES;SEND/RETURN;coaxial DIGITAL OUT;AMP CONTROL;SUB EXP/SUB CTL1,2;MIDI IN/OUT;AC-adapter input. **No USB in complete connector inventory.** | [Product][G8-P] |
| Analog conversion | 24-bit+AF A/D;24-bit D/A;44.1kHz;≥100dB IHF-A dynamic range. | [Product][G8-P] |
| Power / battery / USB | **14V AC,650mA; BRC-series adapter**. AC supply: DC centre polarity is inapplicable. Battery operation unverified; no USB connector. Do not substitute GT-10’s9V DC supply. | [Product][G8-P] |
| IR / NAM / capture | Import capabilities unverified. Factory COSM does not establish loading. | Unresolved |
| Editor / platforms | Official support lists custom patches; full editor/platform compatibility unverified. MIDI data transfer is distinct from USB audio. | [Product][G8-P] |
| Patch switching / live | Smooth mode carries delay/reverb decay only with matching delay/reverb types and effect-chain order; other settings may prevent the desired result. Switching may be delayed. Bank selection normally waits for a number pedal; Bank Change mode can alter this. | [Indexed manual pp16,71][G8-M] |
| Looper / Hold delay | **2.8s maximum**, overdubbing; consumes DELAY’s Hold type. Works only in Play screen. Wait2.8s after selecting Hold. Current number pedal controls it; Manual mode uses the assigned delay pedal. Recording erased by playback stop, type/patch change or power-off. CTL/external Hold Delay Stop returns immediately to standby. | [Manufacturer manual pp31–32][G8-R] |
| Routing / live | Dual amp channels layer/switch with picking dynamics or expression crossfade; independent external loop. Hold looping qualified above; drums/scenes unverified; conditional tails described above. | [Product][G8-P] |

| K/F/B | **23 / 3 / 4**, text-derived: BANK up/down, WRITE, EDIT/EXIT. Toe and rear power switches additional; visual corroboration pending. | [Product][M50B-P]; [Manual pp22–25][M50B-M] |

Master EQ and noise suppression separate. Full capacity accounting pending. | [Product][M50B-P]; [Manual pp8–15,20][M50B-M] |

| Synth / acoustic | Four bass synth choices; single-note restriction. HOLLOW adds acoustic-electric bass body resonance, explicitly documented on p11. This supersedes the earlier unverified acoustic-body entry; not evidence of guitar acoustic simulation or user IR. | [Product][M50B-P]; [Manual pp11–12][M50B-M] |
| I/O | INPUT; L/MONO,R outputs; stereo-mini AUX; PHONES; FOOT SW (COMP ON/OFF,TEMPO); adapter jack. Remaining connector sizes and electrical routing pending. | [Product][M50B-P] |
| Power / battery / USB | 9V DC,140mA; six AA or optional PSA adapter. Manual estimates 12h alkaline/3.5h carbon. Polarity and USB operation unverified. | [Product][M50B-P]; [Manual p30][M50B-M] |
| IR / NAM / capture | User IR, native NAM, converted NAM and capture imports unverified. | Unresolved |
| Editor / platforms | Full patch editor, driver/updater and mobile support unestablished. No software downloaded. | Unresolved |
| Looper / drums / live | HOLD DLY 2.8s with layering; recording erased by stop, type change or power-off. Kick Drum has six tones; automatic beat interval 240–2000ms. Kick/loop quantization documented. Scenes and patch-change spillover pending; effect-bypass delay tails qualified below. | [Manual pp14,16–19][M50B-M] |
| Routing | Order automatically follows settings; free reordering unverified. | [Manual p8][M50B-M] |
| Category / shared-slot limits | FILTER/TONE selects one of 11; octave modes require single notes. DELAY/MOD selects one of 11, including combined chorus+delay and chorus+reverb; their chorus settings are fixed. Not independent chorus, delay and reverb blocks. | [Manual pp10,14–15][M50B-M] |
| Delay / live limits | Analog 100–500ms; short 0–400ms; long 300–1400ms; dotted-tap 46.5–1050ms; chorus+delay 100–800ms. Delay bypass cuts input, allowing feedback tails briefly; not proof of patch-change spillover. | [Manual pp14–15][M50B-M] |
| Drive / routing detail | Hi-band drive distorts highs while retaining lows. With octave-up selected, drive direct-level takes original input; distortion affects the raised octave. | [Manual pp12–13][M50B-M] |
| External footswitch | FS-5U: compressor toggle in Manual, bank-up in Memory. Two via PCS-31: compressor+tempo in Manual, bank up/down in Memory. Tempo pedal stops HOLD DLY immediately; reverb modes ignore it. | [Manual pp26–27][M50B-M] |
| Patch operation | Manual starts at power-up. Memory pedals select patches rather than toggle effects; Patch Edit enables effect toggling. Knobs use value pickup; unsaved edits are lost. | [Manual pp21–24][M50B-M] |
| AUX / tuner | AUX volume is source-controlled. Tuner 435–445Hz; exit Tuner before power-off to save pitch. Bypass retains pedal-volume control. | [Manual pp25,27][M50B-M] |
| Expression calibration | Heel/toe calibration; toe-switch force 1–9, lower is lighter. | [Manual p28][M50B-M] |
| Power / adapter compatibility | Roland’s 2025-01-14 article explicitly permits PSA 200mA or 500mA versions for the ME-50B’s 9V DC/140mA draw. Adapter capacity differs from device consumption; polarity remains unresolved in inspected text. | [Roland power support][M50B-PS] |
| External control / cable | Two FS-5Us require a stereo send/return insert cable, per Roland’s 2025-10-08 ME-50/ME-50B article. This establishes switching cabling, not external expression support. | [Roland cable support][M50B-C] |
| Expression volume limits | Volume-pedal minimum/maximum levels cannot be configured. Roland suggests a louder stored patch for solo boosts. | [Japanese manufacturer FAQ][M50B-E] |
| Tuner / live source conflict | English support says Tuner automatically mutes; manual p25 and Japanese FAQ describe audible dry bypass with pedal-volume muting. Preserve the contradiction; no tested resolution. Japanese FAQ also qualifies a WAH-mode exception. | [English tuner support][M50B-TE]; [Japanese tuner FAQ][M50B-TJ]; [Manual p25][M50B-M] |
| Analog levels / conversion | INPUT/AUX nominal −10dBu; impedance 1Mohm/100kohm respectively. Output −10dBu,2kohm. A/D 24-bit+AF; D/A 24-bit;44.1kHz. | [Product][M50B-P] |

### ME-50B — synth and expression choices

Checked2026-10-18 against [original manual pp12–13,18–19][M50B-M]. Text inspection; no software/manual downloads or new visual control-count verification.

| Field | Finding / qualification |
|---|---|
| Synth / waveform and controls | **SAW LEAD,MILD FILTER,DEEP FILTER use saw-wave synth sounds; SQUARE uses square-wave**. Allfour require single-note playing. DRIVE/SENS controls filtering; TONE/CUTOFF controls cutoff; D.LEVEL/RESONANCE controls resonance insynthmode,not dryblend. Do not transfer thedrive-mode direct-level meaning to synthmode. |
| Expression / six effect choices | **WAH,RESONANCE,+1 OCTAVE,−1 OCTAVE,SOUND HOLD,KICK DRUM**, alternatives tovolume mode. ±octave andSoundHold require single notes. RESONANCE cancombine withSAW LEAD/SQUARE; onepedaleffect isnot sixsimultaneousblocks. |
| Looper / Sound Hold distinction | SoundHold sustains aplayednote andpedalposition adjustsitslevel; raisingthetoe farenough cancelsit. It is distinctfrom2.8s HOLD DLY recording/layering,not anadditional general-purpose looper. |

Humanizer alternates two vowels selected independently from u/o/i/e/a; trigger can restart from vowel 1 on a played string. EQ has parametric mids (63 Hz–11 kHz) and separate Presence. Level ranges −18…18 are displayed parameter values; dB units not established by this passage. | [Manual p22][ME8-M] |
| Processing / harmony or pitch | Harmonist and Pitch Shifter are alternatives. Harmonist requires **single-note playing**, with two harmony levels, direct level and major/minor song key. Pitch Shifter supports chords; two shifted voices each cover **−12…+12 semitones**, with independent Fine −20…20 (units unestablished here). | [Manual pp23–24][ME8-M] |
| Categories / shared modulation | **Nine alternatives**: Chorus, Stereo Chorus, Flanger, Hi Band Flanger, Phaser 1–4, Tremolo. Phaser 1/2/3 use four/eight/ten phases; Phaser 4 chains two phasers. Indexed MODE summary repeats PH2 where the detailed list identifies PH3; preserve that transcription discrepancy. | [Manual p24][ME8-M] |
| Categories / reverb | **Six alternatives**: Hall 1/2, Room 1/2, Plate 1/2. TIME **1–20 is a displayed parameter range**, with seconds not established by the passage; Tone −50…50 and effect level 0…100. | [Manual p27][ME8-M] |
| Expression / control-effect restrictions | **Slow Attack is CTL-only; Foot Volume is EXP-only**. Foot Volume preserves delay/reverb decay when lowering the pre-ambience signal. Feedbacker requires a stable single note; CTL press raises feedback and release mutes it, while EXP varies its level. Intelligent Ring Modulator recommends single notes and remains ring-modulated whenever selected. Pedal Wah supports CTL or continuous EXP. | [Manual p13][ME8-M] |
| Categories / control targets | Assignments include Pedal Wah, Feedbacker, Ring Modulator, Intelligent Ring Modulator, Slow Attack, Foot Volume, Arm Down 1–3, Arm Up 1–2, Trip 1–2, Vibrato, Silent, Tempo, OD/DS, HR/PS, modulation, Delay, Reverb and Tuner; Off additional. Arm Down 1 reaches two octaves down. These are assignable choices, not additional simultaneous blocks. | [Manual p13][ME8-M] |
| Expression / Arm, Trip and Vibrato | Arm Down 2 lowers two semitones; Down 3 one octave; Up 1 raises three semitones; Up 2 one octave. Trip 1 adds a voice up to two octaves above dry; Trip 2 adds one up to an octave below dry. CTL drives timed pitch movement; EXP follows pedal position. Vibrato: CTL gradually applies it while held; EXP varies depth and lifted position disables it. | [Manual p14][ME8-M] |
| Live / Silent, Tempo and remote restrictions | **CTL-only**: Silent mutes all outputs and clears audible delay/reverb tails; Tempo requires more than four presses; Remote toggles drive, harmony/pitch, modulation, delay, reverb or tuner. These targets do not work with EXP. Silent is distinct from Foot Volume, which preserves ambience decay. | [Manual p14][ME8-M] |
| Categories / amp simulator and suppression | Amp simulator selects **Small Box or Large Box**, with Off additional. Noise-suppression threshold spans Off–100; excessive threshold can suppress quiet playing. Master Level 80 gives equal input/output level when all effects are off. CTL and optional EXP have separate assignment parameters. | [Manual p28][ME8-M] |
| Preset memory | **50 user + 50 preset = 100**, plus Manual settings; initial user contents pending. | [Manual p34][ME8-M] |
| Synth / acoustic | Dedicated synthesis/acoustic simulation unverified. | Unresolved |
| I/O | Input; L/Mono and R outputs; stereo mini headphones; external drive send/return; Manual Remote; Tuner Remote; EXP; Tempo In; adapter. | [Manual p34][ME8-M] |
| Power / battery / USB | **14 V AC; 500 mA draw**. Battery/USB operation unverified; AC has no fixed DC center polarity. | [Manual p34][ME8-M] |
| Dimensions / mass | **422 W × 206 D × 55 H mm; 2.2 kg excluding adapter**. | [Manual p34][ME8-M] |
| IR / NAM / capture | Import, native/converted NAM and proprietary capture unverified. | Unresolved |
| Editor / platforms | Editor and current OS support unverified. | Unresolved |
| Live / practice | Manual mode documented; looper, drums, scenes and routing restrictions pending. | [Manual p34][ME8-M] |

## GT-3

Checked 2026-10-17; partial. Guitar/acoustic-electric multi-effects; bass/vocal suitability and ownership unverified.

<!-- comparison evidence begins -->

| Field | Finding / qualification | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| Expression | Built-in expression and CTL; expression function selectable per patch. External shared SUB EXP/SUB CTL1,2 supports EV-5 or FV-300L + PCS-33; toe support pending. | [Manual p5][GT3-M] |
| K/F/B | **2 rotary / 7 footswitches / button total unresolved**. Specifications enumerate 19 button functions, but the Easy Edit passage names further controls whose physical sharing needs reconciliation. Text-derived inventory: Value and rear Output Level; Number1–4, Bank Up/Down, CTL; 12 effect-select, Exit, Parameter L/R, Write, Manual, Utility, Tuner/Bypass. Power switch separate; visual corroboration pending. | [Manual p71][GT3-M] |
| Display | **16-character × 2-line backlit LCD**; diagonal, color and touch pending. | [Manual p71][GT3-M] |
| Processing / categories | Official product says **32 effects**, manual introduction **31**; conflict retained. Simultaneous ceiling pending. COSM preamp and speaker/mic simulation. Product advertises **12 analog drive effects**; manual p43 instead lists **11 internal types plus External**. Preserve this counting distinction. | [Product][GT3-P]; [Manual p5][GT3-M] |
| Editor / onboard Easy Edit | Hardware editing offers Type and Variation plus four sound adjustments: Drive, Tone, Modulation and Delay. Patch Data preserves corresponding original settings; Variation cannot change when Type is Patch Data. Save with Write before relying on edits. This is onboard editing, not evidence of computer-editor support. | [Manual p19][GT3-M] |
| Processing / documented combinations | Manufacturer patch list documents **Harmonist + Slow Gear** (32-3), **Auto Wah + Phaser + Panner** (33-3), and **Step Phaser + Panner + Delay** (34-4). Patch 35-4 places Phaser before the preamp. These establish particular combinations/routing, not a global simultaneous ceiling or every possible combination. | [Official patch list p4][GT3-L] |
| Live / combined CTL assignment | Patch 34-2 uses CTL to change preamp volume and add delay together. Patch 31-4 synchronizes Slicer and Panner speed through Master BPM. Factory patch examples establish documented assignments, not arbitrary unlimited target counts. | [Official patch list p4][GT3-L] |
| Categories / OD/DS | **11 internal alternatives**: Natural OD, Vintage OD, Turbo OD, Blues, Crunch, Distortion 1/2, Grunge, Metal 1/2, Fuzz. **EXT OD/DS** selects connected external hardware; it is not a twelfth internal distortion. Drive and Level 0–100; Bass/Treble −50…50 displayed values, units not specified here. | [Manual p43][GT3-M] |
| Categories / MOD selector | **12 alternatives**: Harmonist, Flanger, Phaser, Auto Riff, Sub EQ, 2×2 Chorus, Short Delay, Humanizer, Vibrato, Guitar Synth, Ring Modulator, Slicer (HR/FL/PH/AR/SEQ/2CE/SDD/HU/VB/SYN/RM/SL). One FX Select list does not represent 12 simultaneous MOD blocks. | [Manual p46][GT3-M] |
| Categories / MOD subchoices | Harmonist offers 1-Voice, 2-Mono or 2-Stereo. Phaser: 4/8/12-stage or Bi-Phase. Auto Riff: **30 preset + 10 user phrases**, separate from patch memory. Short Delay: **0–400 ms**. Humanizer: Picking/Auto/Random. Synth: Square/Saw/Brass/Bow; chromatic, octave shift and Hold apply only to Square/Saw. Ring Modulator: Normal/Intelligent. | [Manual p46][GT3-M] |
| Categories / SFX | **Six selector alternatives**: Acoustic Guitar Simulator, Slow Gear, Anti-feedback, Feedbacker, Pickup Simulator, Tremolo/Pan. These share SFX selection. Pan requires stereo output for left/right movement. Feedbacker requires accurate single notes before engaging. | [Manual p39][GT3-M] |
| Live / anti-feedback search | Automatic search sets feedback-cancellation frequencies; results clear on patch change. **Other operations suspend during search**, potentially interrupting Auto Riff phrases. Manual recommends assigning search to CTL; manual frequency/depth settings are also available. | [Manual p39][GT3-M] |
| Categories / reverb | **Five alternatives**: Room 1/2, Hall 1/2, Plate. Reverb time **0.1–10.0 seconds**; pre-delay **0–100 ms**; low cut 55–800 Hz; high cut 700 Hz–11 kHz or Flat; density 0–10. These are choices/settings within the reverb section. | [Manual p56][GT3-M] |
| Categories / dedicated chorus | **Mono or Stereo**: Mono sends the same chorus to L/R; Stereo sends different chorus signals. Pre-delay **0–40 ms**; Rate/Depth/Level 0–100; BPM-synchronized rate available. Depth 0 supports doubling. High Cut table lists 700 Hz–11 kHz/Flat, but its explanatory paragraph describes low-cut behavior; source inconsistency retained. This section is separate from MOD 2×2 Chorus. | [Manual p55][GT3-M] |
| Categories / preamp | **14 alternatives**: JC-120, Clean Twin, Crunch, Match Drive, Voxy Drive, Blues, BG Lead, MS1959 I/II/I+II, SLDN Lead, Metal 5150, Metal Drive, AC Guitar. AC Guitar is an acoustic-electric preamp, distinct from SFX acoustic simulation. Speaker simulation activates with global Line (Headphones); AC Guitar flat-amp simulation instead switches off in that setting. | [Manual p44][GT3-M] |
| Processing / preamp control exceptions | Match Drive ignores Middle. Match/Voxy Presence works as a high-frequency cut, displayed 0…−100. Depending on type, setting Bass/Middle/Treble all to zero may silence output. These are model-specific control behaviors. | [Manual p44][GT3-M] |
| Processing / programmable effect order | Manual explicitly permits changing effect connection order through Master → Effect Chain; insert the chosen processor at the cursor. Noise Suppressor is selected through Master and Foot Volume through Pedal/Assign. Save the resulting order using Write. This proves programmable order, not duplicated instances or a numerical simultaneous ceiling. | [Manual section 2, connection-order procedure][GT3-M] |
| Categories / delay | **Single or Tap**; Tap divides delay between stereo outputs. Delay time **0–1800 ms**, fine adjustment **0–20 ms**, with per-patch Master BPM synchronization. Tap Time **0–100%** sets right-channel time relative to left at 100%. Feedback 0–100; high cut 700 Hz–11 kHz or Flat; effect level 0–120. | [Manual pp54–55][GT3-M] |
| Processing / intelligent ring restriction | Intelligent Ring Modulator follows detected input pitch and requires single-note playing. Normal mode instead uses the internal oscillator. | [Manual p54][GT3-M] |
| Live / control assignments | **Eight assignments per patch** map controllers to parameters with target minimum/maximum, source mode and active range. **Four user assignment settings** can be reused across patches; these are not four extra sound patches. Save changes with Write. | [Manual p26][GT3-M] |
| Expression / virtual pedal triggers | Internal and Wave Pedal are virtual controllers. Internal Pedal can trigger on patch selection, built-in expression low/mid/high positions, CTL, external expression or SUB CTL1/2. Position thresholds do not establish a physical toe switch. Momentary footswitches support hold-dependent control; latch switches cannot provide that behavior. | [Manual section 2, Internal Pedal / active range][GT3-M] |
| Preset memory | **140 user + 200 preset = 340**; initial user contents pending. | [Manual p71][GT3-M] |
| Synth / acoustic | Guitar synth and acoustic simulation documented; Auto Riff triggers riffs from a note. Acoustic-electric preamp and anti-feedback also documented. | [Manual p5][GT3-M]; [Product][GT3-P] |
| I/O | Input, L/Mono and R outputs, stereo mini headphones, send/return, shared SUB EXP/SUB CTL1,2, MIDI In/Out; adapter input. | [Manual p71][GT3-M] |
| Power / battery / USB | **14 V AC; 800 mA draw**, supplied BRC-120/230/240 adapter variants. AC has no fixed DC center polarity; battery/USB operation unverified. | [Manual p71][GT3-M] |
| Dimensions / mass | **487 W × 222 D × 97 H mm; 4.0 kg**; adapter inclusion in mass not specified in this passage. | [Manual p71][GT3-M] |
| IR / NAM / capture | Import, native/converted NAM and proprietary capture pending. | Unresolved |
| Editor / platforms | Editor and current OS support pending. | Unresolved |
| Live / practice | Virtual internal pedal controls parameter movement; tuner documented. Looper/drums/scenes and detailed routing pending. | [Manual p5][GT3-M] |

### AD-10 — manual routing and memory qualifications

Checked2026-10-17 against [official English manual][AD10-PDF], file eng04_W,14pages, ©2017 Roland; printed pp2–3,5,9–13.

| Field | Verified behavior / gaps |
|---|---|
| I/O / output routing | XLR andLINE independently select MIX,PRE/POST,orCH. MIX includes USB playback; PRE/POST sends pre-effectsL/processedR; CH sends input2L/input1R. Ambience can feed both output groups,either,or neither. |
| Processing / input2 bypass | Input2 FX BYPASS retains **looper and ambience**. Mono send/return;direct mixing selectable when RETURN is connected. Not a fully dry bypass. |
| Factory / user memory | Ten editable effect/setup memories. Startup defaultsManual,configurable toMemory orMemoryStomp. Per-effect memory/manual selection allows some controls to remain at knob positions across recalls. Factory-filled count unknown. |
| Preset controls / saving | HoldEXIT+MEMORY,confirmMEMORY,choose destination,confirmagain. Recalled knobs use pickup behavior: value changes only after crossing the stored position. |
| USB audio / monitoring | USB direct-outOFF sends unit sound only tocomputer;host-processed return can feed outputs. ON restores local output. Dedicated driver required;not evidence of a full editor orUSB power. |
| Expression / live | External EXP targets output,ambience,orloop level. Three named footswitches: BOOST,DELAY,TUNER&MUTE;rotary/button inventory is documented below. |

### ME-5 — official indexed manual recovery

Checked2026-10-17. Direct [official PDF][ME5-M] still haszero extracted lines,but targeted search returned its own original section “Function of each parameter”,printed p36.
